    SELECT [m].[Id], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], CASE
        WHEN [t].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
        ELSE [t].[TotalCount]
    END AS [c]
    FROM [Manga] AS [m]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[MangaId], [v].[TotalCount]
        FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]
        WHERE ([v].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [v].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [v].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ) AS [t] ON [m].[Id] = [t].[MangaId]
    WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ORDER BY CASE
        WHEN [t].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
        ELSE [t].[TotalCount]
    END DESC, [m].[Id] DESC
    OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t0]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[TagId] AS [Id], [t2].[Name], [t2].[Description], [m0].[MangaId]
    FROM [MangaTag] AS [m0]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Description], [t3].[Name]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE ([t3].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [t3].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [t3].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ) AS [t2] ON [m0].[TagId] = [t2].[Id]
    WHERE ([m0].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m0].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m0].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t1] ON [t0].[Id] = [t1].[MangaId]
ORDER BY [t0].[c] DESC, [t0].[Id] DESC, [t0].[Id0]

    WHEN [t0].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
    ELSE [t0].[TotalCount]
END, COALESCE((
    SELECT AVG([r].[Rating])
    FROM [Rating] AS [r]

    SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[LastChapterId], [m].[LastChapterIndex], [m].[LastChapterUpdate], [m].[MangaBackground], [m].[MangaThumbnail], [m].[Name], [m].[SeasonName]
    FROM [Manga] AS [m]
    WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL

    OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]

) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[MangaId]

    WHEN [t0].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
    ELSE [t0].[TotalCount]
END, COALESCE((
    SELECT AVG([r].[Rating])
    FROM [Rating] AS [r]
    WHERE ([r].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [t].[Id] = [r].[ToMangaId]), 0.0E0), [t0].[Id]
FROM (
    SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[LastChapterId], [m].[LastChapterIndex], [m].[LastChapterUpdate], [m].[MangaBackground], [m].[MangaThumbnail], [m].[Name], [m].[SeasonName]
    FROM [Manga] AS [m]
    WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ORDER BY [m].[LastChapterUpdate] DESC, [m].[Id] DESC
    OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]

    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[MangaId], [v].[TotalCount]
    FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]

) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[MangaId]

    WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ORDER BY [m].[LastChapterUpdate] DESC, [m].[Id] DESC
    OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[MangaId]
    FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]
    WHERE ([v].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [v].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [v].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[MangaId]

    SELECT [m0].[TagId] AS [Id], [t2].[Name], [t2].[Description], [m0].[MangaId]
    FROM [MangaTag] AS [m0]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Description], [t3].[Name]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE ([t3].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [t3].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [t3].[DeleteDate] IS NULL

) AS [t1] ON [t].[Id] = [t1].[MangaId]
ORDER BY [t].[LastChapterUpdate] DESC, [t].[Id] DESC, [t0].[Id]

    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END, [t2].[Id], [t2].[MangaId], [t2].[Index], [t2].[Title], [t2].[CreateDate], CASE

    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END, [t3].[Id], [t3].[DisplayName], [t3].[AvatarThumbnail]

    ORDER BY [r].[Rating]
    OFFSET @__p_1 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [u].[Id], [u].[DisplayName]
    FROM [Users] AS [u]
    WHERE ([u].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [u].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [u].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
